Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in approximation theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 240 and the invariant I(A,B) = 854 mod 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 240 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the approximation structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 854. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
